## GraphQL Schema Analysis: createArticle Mutation Status

Based on my thorough exploration of the HackersPub Android project, here are my findings:

### **CRITICAL FINDING: No Direct createArticle Mutation Exists**

The GraphQL schema does **NOT** have a `createArticle` mutation. Instead, the schema implements a **draft-based workflow** for article creation:

### Available Article-Related Mutations

The schema defines these article mutations in `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/graphql/pub/hackers/android/schema.graphqls`:

1. **saveArticleDraft** (line 936)
   - Input: `SaveArticleDraftInput`
   - Returns: `SaveArticleDraftResult`
   - Purpose: Create or update an article draft

2. **publishArticleDraft** (line 919)
   - Input: `PublishArticleDraftInput`
   - Returns: `PublishArticleDraftResult`
   - Purpose: Publish a draft to create a published Article

3. **deleteArticleDraft** (line 899)
   - Input: `DeleteArticleDraftInput`
   - Returns: `DeleteArticleDraftResult`
   - Purpose: Delete an unpublished draft

### Input Types

**SaveArticleDraftInput** (lines 1727-1737):
```
- clientMutationId: ID (optional)
- content: Markdown! (required)
- id: ID (optional - for updating existing drafts)
- tags: [String!]! (required)
- title: String! (required)
```

**PublishArticleDraftInput** (lines 1347-1357):
```
- allowLlmTranslation: Boolean (optional)
- clientMutationId: ID (optional)
- id: ID! (required - the draft ID to publish)
- language: Locale! (required)
- slug: String! (required)
```

**DeleteArticleDraftInput** (lines 616-620):
```
- clientMutationId: ID (optional)
- id: ID! (required)
```

### Return Types

**SaveArticleDraftPayload**:
```
- clientMutationId: ID
- draft: ArticleDraft!
```

**PublishArticleDraftPayload**:
```
- article: Article!
- clientMutationId: ID
- deletedDraftId: ID!
```

### Article Types Available

**ArticleDraft** (lines 525-546):
```
- account: Account!
- content: Markdown!
- contentHtml: HTML!
- created: DateTime!
- id: ID!
- tags: [String!]!
- title: String!
- updated: DateTime!
- uuid: UUID!
```

**Article** (lines 424-490) - The published post type:
- Implements: Node, Post, Reactable interfaces
- Contains: All post fields plus article-specific properties like slug, publishedYear, language, etc.

### Operations File Status

The `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/graphql/pub/hackers/android/operations.graphql` file contains **no article-related mutations** currently. It has queries for actors, posts, timelines, and basic mutations for notes, follow/block actions, and reactions, but no mutation operations for article creation or draft management.

### Conclusion

**If you need to add `createArticle` functionality:**
- You could either: 
  1. Add a direct `createArticle` mutation to the backend schema that bypasses the draft workflow
  2. Implement the UI to use the existing `saveArticleDraft` → `publishArticleDraft` workflow
  
The current schema implements option 2 (draft-based workflow), which is actually more flexible for long-form content. No schema changes are needed if you adopt this approach.
```

## FAB and Compose Navigation Flow Analysis

Based on my exploration of the HackersPub Android app, here's the complete flow:

### Key File Paths:
1. **Navigation Hub**: `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/HackersPubApp.kt`
2. **Timeline FAB**: `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/timeline/TimelineScreen.kt`
3. **Compose Screen**: `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/compose/ComposeScreen.kt`
4. **Compose ViewModel**: `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/compose/ComposeViewModel.kt`
5. **Repository**: `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/repository/HackersPubRepository.kt`
6. **GraphQL Operations**: `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/graphql/pub/hackers/android/operations.graphql`

### Current FAB and Navigation Flow Architecture:

#### 1. **FAB Implementation in TimelineScreen** (Lines 176-187)
The FAB is a standard Material3 `FloatingActionButton` with:
- **Icon**: `Icons.Default.Add` (plus icon)
- **Color**: `colors.composeAccent` (container), white content
- **OnClick Callback**: `onComposeClick(null)` - passes null to indicate new post, not a reply

```
TimelineScreen FAB onClick
    ↓
onComposeClick(null)
    ↓
navController.navigate(DetailScreen.Compose.createRoute(null))
```

#### 2. **DetailScreen Route Definition** (HackersPubApp.kt, Lines 102-126)
```kotlin
sealed class DetailScreen(val route: String) {
    data object Compose : DetailScreen("compose?replyTo={replyTo}&quoteOf={quoteOf}") {
        fun createRoute(replyTo: String? = null, quoteOf: String? = null): String {
            val params = mutableListOf<String>()
            if (replyTo != null) params.add("replyTo=$replyTo")
            if (quoteOf != null) params.add("quoteOf=$quoteOf")
            return if (params.isEmpty()) "compose" else "compose?${params.joinToString("&")}"
        }
    }
    // Other detail screens...
}
```

**Usage variants**:
- New post: `DetailScreen.Compose.createRoute()` → `"compose"`
- Reply: `DetailScreen.Compose.createRoute(replyTo = postId)` → `"compose?replyTo=postId"`
- Quote: `DetailScreen.Compose.createRoute(quoteOf = postId)` → `"compose?quoteOf=postId"`

#### 3. **Navigation Graph in HackersPubApp** (Lines 417-447)
The compose route is registered with 2 optional arguments:
```kotlin
composable(
    route = DetailScreen.Compose.route,
    arguments = listOf(
        navArgument("replyTo") {
            type = NavType.StringType
            nullable = true
            defaultValue = null
        },
        navArgument("quoteOf") {
            type = NavType.StringType
            nullable = true
            defaultValue = null
        }
    )
) { backStackEntry ->
    val replyTo = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("replyTo")
    val quoteOf = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("quoteOf")
    ComposeScreen(
        replyToId = replyTo,
        quotedPostId = quoteOf,
        onPostSuccess = { /* refresh previous screen */ },
        onNavigateBack = { navController.popBackStack() }
    )
}
```

#### 4. **ComposeScreen Initialization** (ComposeScreen.kt, Lines 88-94)
```kotlin
@Composable
fun ComposeScreen(
    replyToId: String?,
    quotedPostId: String? = null,
    onPostSuccess: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateBack: () -> Unit,
    viewModel: ComposeViewModel = hiltViewModel()
)
```

#### 5. **ComposeViewModel State Management** (ComposeViewModel.kt)
Main UI state (`ComposeUiState`, Lines 27-47):
- `content: String` - post text content
- `cursorPosition: Int` - for mention autocomplete
- `visibility: PostVisibility` - PUBLIC/UNLISTED/FOLLOWERS/DIRECT
- `replyToId: String?` - parent post ID
- `replyTargetPost: Post?` - loaded parent post for preview
- `quotedPostId: String?` - quoted post ID
- `quotedPost: Post?` - loaded quoted post for preview
- `isPosting: Boolean` - submission in progress
- `isPosted: Boolean` - success flag
- `mentionSuggestions: List<Actor>` - autocomplete suggestions

#### 6. **Compose Flow - Reply/Quote Setup**
When `replyToId` or `quotedPostId` is provided:
- `LaunchedEffect(replyToId)` (ComposeScreen.kt, Line 144) → calls `viewModel.setReplyTarget(replyTo)`
- `LaunchedEffect(quotedPostId)` (ComposeScreen.kt, Line 148) → calls `viewModel.setQuotedPost(quotedPostId)`

In ViewModel (ComposeViewModel.kt, Lines 209-229):
```kotlin
fun setReplyTarget(postId: String) {
    // Loads the post, builds mention prefix, auto-focuses text field
    repository.getPostDetail(postId)
        .onSuccess { result ->
            val mentionPrefix = buildMentionPrefix(result.post)
            // Updates state with post + auto-mentions author
        }
}
```

#### 7. **Post Creation** (ComposeViewModel.kt, Lines 286-312)
```kotlin
fun post() {
    repository.createNote(
        content = state.content,
        language = state.language,
        visibility = state.visibility,
        replyTargetId = state.replyToId,
        quotedPostId = state.quotedPostId
    ).onSuccess {
        _uiState.update { it.copy(isPosting = false, isPosted = true) }
        // Triggers onPostSuccess callback
    }
}
```

#### 8. **Post Success Handling** (HackersPubApp.kt, Lines 437-441)
```kotlin
onPostSuccess = {
    navController.previousBackStackEntry
        ?.savedStateHandle
        ?.set("postedAt", System.currentTimeMillis())
    navController.popBackStack()
}
```
- Sets `postedAt` timestamp on previous screen's saved state
- TimelineScreen listens for this (Lines 74-83) and refreshes feed

### Call Paths from Different Sources:

**From Timeline FAB**:
```
TimelineScreen.onComposeClick(null)
  → navController.navigate("compose")
  → ComposeScreen(replyToId=null, quotedPostId=null)
```

**From Post Reply Button**:
```
PostCard.onReplyClick(postId)
  → TimelineScreen.onComposeClick(postId)
  → navController.navigate("compose?replyTo=$postId")
  → ComposeScreen(replyToId=postId, quotedPostId=null)
```

**From Post Quote Button**:
```
PostCard.onQuoteClick(postId)
  → TimelineScreen.onQuoteClick(postId)
  → navController.navigate("compose?quoteOf=$postId")
  → ComposeScreen(replyToId=null, quotedPostId=postId)
```

### Key Features:
- **No SpeedDial or ExpandableFAB pattern** - Single simple FAB for compose only
- **Deep linking support** - Routes can be called from notifications with pre-filled parameters
- **Context-aware UI** - ComposeScreen shows reply/quote previews based on parameters
- **Refresh coordination** - Successfully posted content triggers timeline refresh via savedStateHandle
- **Mention autocomplete** - Built into compose with debounced search (300ms)
- **Language detection** - Automatic language detection for posted content

This architecture provides a clean, type-safe navigation pattern perfect for extending with article creation - you can follow the same `DetailScreen` sealed class pattern and route registration approach.
```

I now have a comprehensive understanding of the entire codebase. Let me design the implementation plan.

---

## Implementation Plan: Add "Create Article" to Home Screen

### 1. FAB UX Decision: Bottom Sheet Selection

**Recommendation: Option B -- Bottom Sheet when FAB is tapped.**

Rationale:
- The codebase already uses `ModalBottomSheet` in `TimelineScreen.kt` (the reaction picker at line 115). This is an established pattern.
- A speed-dial FAB requires bringing in an external library or building custom animation logic, adding complexity for an MVP.
- A bottom sheet is the most conventional Android pattern for "choose an action type" and requires no new dependencies.
- The existing `ModalBottomSheet` import is already in `TimelineScreen.kt`.

The FAB tap would show a bottom sheet with two rows: "New Note" (with a short-text icon) and "New Article" (with an article/document icon). Tapping either dismisses the sheet and navigates to the appropriate compose screen.

### 2. Workflow Decision: Simplified "Save Draft then Publish" in One Flow

For MVP, the article creation screen should handle the two-step API workflow (saveArticleDraft then publishArticleDraft) behind a single "Publish" button. There is no need for a separate draft management screen at this stage. The user fills in all fields on one screen, and the app:
1. Calls `saveArticleDraft` to persist the draft
2. Immediately calls `publishArticleDraft` with the returned draft ID

Optionally, a "Save Draft" button can be offered for users who want to save without publishing, but the primary action should be "Publish."

### 3. Detailed Step-by-Step Plan

#### Step 3.1: Add GraphQL Operations

**File: `/app/src/main/graphql/pub/hackers/android/operations.graphql`**

Add two new mutations at the bottom of the file:

```graphql
mutation SaveArticleDraft($title: String!, $content: Markdown!, $tags: [String!]!, $id: ID) {
    saveArticleDraft(input: { title: $title, content: $content, tags: $tags, id: $id }) {
        ... on SaveArticleDraftPayload {
            draft {
                id
                uuid
                title
                content
                tags
                created
                updated
            }
        }
        ... on InvalidInputError {
            inputPath
        }
        ... on NotAuthenticatedError {
            notAuthenticated
        }
    }
}

mutation PublishArticleDraft($id: ID!, $language: Locale!, $slug: String!, $allowLlmTranslation: Boolean) {
    publishArticleDraft(input: { id: $id, language: $language, slug: $slug, allowLlmTranslation: $allowLlmTranslation }) {
        ... on PublishArticleDraftPayload {
            article {
                id
            }
            deletedDraftId
        }
        ... on InvalidInputError {
            inputPath
        }
        ... on NotAuthenticatedError {
            notAuthenticated
        }
    }
}
```

This follows the exact same pattern as the existing `CreateNote` mutation (lines 509-525 of `operations.graphql`) -- using inline fragments for the union result type with `InvalidInputError` and `NotAuthenticatedError` branches.

#### Step 3.2: Add Domain Model for ArticleDraft

**File: `/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/domain/model/Models.kt`**

Add to the bottom of the file:

```kotlin
data class ArticleDraft(
    val id: String,
    val uuid: String,
    val title: String,
    val content: String,
    val tags: List<String>,
    val created: Instant,
    val updated: Instant
)

data class ArticlePublishResult(
    val articleId: String,
    val deletedDraftId: String
)
```

#### Step 3.3: Add Repository Methods

**File: `/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/repository/HackersPubRepository.kt`**

Add two new methods following the same pattern as `createNote()` (lines 609-660):

1. `suspend fun saveArticleDraft(title: String, content: String, tags: List<String>, id: String? = null): Result<ArticleDraft>` -- calls the `SaveArticleDraftMutation` Apollo generated class, maps the response to the domain `ArticleDraft` model.

2. `suspend fun publishArticleDraft(id: String, language: String, slug: String, allowLlmTranslation: Boolean = false): Result<ArticlePublishResult>` -- calls the `PublishArticleDraftMutation` Apollo generated class, maps the response to `ArticlePublishResult`.

Both methods should follow the same try/catch + `response.hasErrors()` + union type matching pattern used by `createNote()`. Import the new Apollo-generated mutation classes (e.g., `pub.hackers.android.graphql.SaveArticleDraftMutation`, `pub.hackers.android.graphql.PublishArticleDraftMutation`).

#### Step 3.5: Create ComposeArticleViewModel

**New file: `/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleViewModel.kt`**

This follows the exact structure of `ComposeViewModel.kt`:

- `@HiltViewModel` annotation with `@Inject constructor`
- Injects `HackersPubRepository` and `@ApplicationContext Context` (same as `ComposeViewModel`)
- `ComposeArticleUiState` data class with fields:
  - `title: String = ""`
  - `content: String = ""`
  - `tags: String = ""` (comma-separated string, parsed to list on save)
  - `slug: String = ""` (auto-generated from title, but editable)
  - `language: String = java.util.Locale.getDefault().language`
  - `allowLlmTranslation: Boolean = false`
  - `draftId: String? = null` (set after first save)
  - `isSaving: Boolean = false`
  - `isPublishing: Boolean = false`
  - `isPublished: Boolean = false`
  - `error: String? = null`
- Key methods:
  - `updateTitle(title: String)` -- also auto-generates slug using a simple `title.lowercase().replace(Regex("[^a-z0-9]+"), "-").trim('-')` function
  - `updateContent(content: String)`
  - `updateTags(tags: String)`
  - `updateSlug(slug: String)` -- manual override
  - `updateLanguage(language: String)`
  - `saveDraft()` -- calls `repository.saveArticleDraft()`, stores returned `draftId` in state
  - `publish()` -- calls `saveDraft()` first if no draftId, then calls `repository.publishArticleDraft()` with the draftId, slug, and language. Sets `isPublished = true` on success.
  - `clearError()`
- Reuse the same language detection logic from `ComposeViewModel` (lines 260-280)

#### Step 3.6: Create ComposeArticleScreen

**New file: `/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleScreen.kt`**

This follows the UI structure of `ComposeScreen.kt` but with article-specific fields. Layout:

- **Top bar**: Close button (left), title "Write Article" (center) -- same pattern as `ComposeScreen` lines 173-197
- **Scrollable content area**:

  - Tags `BasicTextField` (single line, comma-separated input)
  - Content `BasicTextField` (multi-line, fills remaining space) -- reuse the same `Surface` + `BasicTextField` pattern from `ComposeScreen` lines 218-286
- **Bottom bar** (pinned above keyboard, like `ComposeScreen` lines 336-456):
  - Slug text field (compact, showing the slug that will be used in the URL)
  - "Save Draft" `TextButton` (secondary action)
  - "Publish" `Button` (primary action, using `colors.composeAccent`)

The screen receives two callback parameters: `onPublishSuccess: () -> Unit` and `onNavigateBack: () -> Unit`, matching the pattern of `ComposeScreen`.

### 4. File Summary

**Files to create (2):**
- `/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleScreen.kt`
- `/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleViewModel.kt`

**Files to modify (6):**
- `/app/src/main/graphql/pub/hackers/android/operations.graphql` -- add 2 mutations
- `/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/domain/model/Models.kt` -- add 2 data classes
- `/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/repository/HackersPubRepository.kt` -- add 2 methods
- `/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml` -- add ~15 strings
- `/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/timeline/TimelineScreen.kt` -- modify FAB, add bottom sheet
- `/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/HackersPubApp.kt` -- add route + navigation wiring

### 5. Potential Challenges

1. **Apollo code generation**: After adding the GraphQL mutations in `operations.graphql`, a build is needed for Apollo to generate `SaveArticleDraftMutation` and `PublishArticleDraftMutation` Kotlin classes. The repository code depends on these generated classes.

2. **Slug validation**: The server may reject certain slug formats. For MVP, auto-generating from the title with a simple regex replacement is sufficient. Server-side validation errors will be surfaced through the existing error handling pattern.

3. **Locale scalar mapping**: The `Locale` scalar is already used by `CreateNote` in `operations.graphql` (line 509: `$language: Locale!`) and handled as a plain `String` in the repository. The same approach works for `publishArticleDraft`.

4. **Two-step API call atomicity**: If `saveArticleDraft` succeeds but `publishArticleDraft` fails, the draft exists on the server but is not published. The ViewModel should store the `draftId` in state so a retry of "Publish" does not create a duplicate draft -- it reuses the existing draft ID.

5. **Bottom sheet import**: `ModalBottomSheet` and `rememberModalBottomSheetState` are already imported in `TimelineScreen.kt` (lines 22-23, 25), so no new imports are needed for the sheet itself. Only `mutableStateOf` and `setValue`/`getValue` delegates need to be ensured (they are already imported via `remember`).

### Critical Files for Implementation
- `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/graphql/pub/hackers/android/operations.graphql`
- `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/repository/HackersPubRepository.kt`
- `/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/timeline/TimelineScreen.kt`

{
  "file_path": "/Users/kodingwarrior/.claude/plans/purrfect-crunching-whale.md",
  "content": "# Add \"Create Article\" Button to Home Screen\n\n## Context\n\nThe home screen (TimelineScreen) currently has a single FAB that goes straight to note composition. We need to add the ability to create **articles** (long-form content with title, tags, slug). The GraphQL schema already supports a draft-based workflow (`saveArticleDraft` -> `publishArticleDraft`), but no operations or UI exist yet.\n\n## UX Flow\n\n### 1. FAB -> Bottom Sheet Chooser\n\nTapping the FAB (+) on the timeline opens a bottom sheet with two options:\n- **New Note** (pencil icon) -> navigates to existing ComposeScreen\n- **New Article** (document icon) -> navigates to new ComposeArticleScreen\n\nThis reuses the `ModalBottomSheet` pattern already in TimelineScreen (reaction picker).\n\n### 2. Article Compose Screen\n\nA full-screen editor with:\n- **Top bar**: Back button + \"Write Article\" title\n- **Title field**: Large, single-line text input\n- **Tags field**: Comma-separated tag input\n- **Content area**: Multi-line markdown editor (fills remaining space)\n- **Bottom bar** (pinned above keyboard):\n  - Slug field (auto-generated from title, editable)\n  - Language selector (reuse existing pattern from ComposeViewModel)\n  - \"Save Draft\" button (secondary)\n  - \"Publish\" button (primary, accent color)\n\n### 3. Publish Flow\n\nTapping \"Publish\":\n1. Calls `saveArticleDraft` to persist the draft\n2. Immediately calls `publishArticleDraft` with the returned draft ID + slug + language\n3. On success, navigates back to timeline and triggers refresh (same `postedAt` pattern as notes)\n\nTapping \"Save Draft\":\n1. Calls `saveArticleDraft` only\n2. Shows a toast/snackbar confirming the draft was saved\n3. Stays on the screen (user can keep editing or navigate back)\n\n## Implementation Steps\n\n### Step 1: GraphQL Operations\n**Modify**: `app/src/main/graphql/pub/hackers/android/operations.graphql`\n- Add `SaveArticleDraft` mutation (title, content, tags, optional id)\n- Add `PublishArticleDraft` mutation (id, language, slug, allowLlmTranslation)\n- Follow the same union result type pattern as `CreateNote`\n\n### Step 2: Domain Models\n**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/domain/model/Models.kt`\n- Add `ArticleDraft` data class (id, uuid, title, content, tags, created, updated)\n- Add `ArticlePublishResult` data class (articleId, deletedDraftId)\n\n### Step 3: Repository Methods\n**Modify**: tap goes to note compose (zero friction for common action)\n- **Top bar icon** for articles — document icon in the top bar navigates directly to a blank article editor\n- **Draft badge** on the article icon — shows draft count so user knows they have unfinished work\n- **Drafts list** — standalone screen accessible from Settings (\"My Drafts (N)\") and by tapping the badge\n- **No auto-save** — user explicitly taps \"Save Draft\"\n\n## Screen Flows\n\n### Timeline with Article Icon + Draft Badge\n\n```\n+----------------------------------+\n| Timeline [doc(2)][👤+][⚙] avatar|\n|                ^^^               |\n|          badge = 2 drafts        |\n|                                  |\n|  +----------------------------+  |\n|  | post...                    |  |\n|  +----------------------------+  |\n|                          [+ FAB] |\n+----------------------------------+\n\n[doc] tap (no drafts)  -> Article Editor (blank)\n[doc] tap (has drafts) -> Article Editor (blank)\n  * Badge is informational; tap always opens blank editor\n  * User goes to Settings > My Drafts to resume drafts\n[+ FAB] tap -> Compose Note (unchanged)\n```\n\n### Article Editor\n\n```\n+----------------------------------+\n| <-  Write Article                |\n|                                  |\n| Title: [___________________]     |\n| Tags:  [___________________]     |\n|                                  |\n| [                              ] |\n| [   Markdown content area      ] |\n| [                              ] |\n|                                  |\n| Slug: [auto-from-title________]  |\n| Lang: [en v]                     |\n| [Save Draft]          [Publish]  |\n+----------------------------------+\n\n\"Publish\" -> saveArticleDraft then publishArticleDraft\n           -> back to timeline + refresh\n\"Save Draft\" -> saveArticleDraft only\n             -> snackbar \"Draft saved\", stay on screen\n```\n\n### My Drafts Screen\n\n```\n+----------------------------------+\n| <-  My Drafts                    |\n|                                  |\n| +------------------------------+ |\n| | \"My WIP article\"             | |\n| | Updated: 2 hours ago   [del] | |\n| +------------------------------+ |\n| | \"Another idea\"               | |\n| | Updated: yesterday     [del] | |\n| +------------------------------+ |\n|                                  |\n| (empty state: \"No drafts yet\")  |\n+----------------------------------+\n\nTap draft -> Article Editor pre-filled with draft content\n[del] -> confirm dialog -> deleteArticleDraft\n```\n\n### Settings Entry\n\n```\nSettings Screen:\n+----------------------------------+\n|  ...                             |\n|  My Drafts (3)              >    |\n|  ...                             |\n+----------------------------------+\n\nTap -> navigate to My Drafts screen\n```\n\n## Implementation Steps\n\n### Step 1: GraphQL Operations\n**Modify**: `app/src/main/graphql/pub/hackers/android/operations.graphql`\n- Add `SaveArticleDraft` mutation (title, content, tags, optional id)\n- Add `PublishArticleDraft` mutation (id, language, slug)\n- Add `DeleteArticleDraft` mutation (id)\n- Add `ArticleDrafts` query (to list user's drafts)\n- Follow union result type pattern from existing `CreateNote`\n\n### Step 2: Domain Models\n**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/domain/model/Models.kt`\n- Add `ArticleDraft(id, title, content, tags, created, updated)`\n- Add `ArticlePublishResult(articleId, deletedDraftId)`\n\n### Step 3: Repository Methods\n**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/repository/HackersPubRepository.kt`\n- `saveArticleDraft(title, content, tags, id?)` -> `Result<ArticleDraft>`\n- `publishArticleDraft(id, language, slug)` -> `Result<ArticlePublishResult>`\n- `deleteArticleDraft(id)` -> `Result<Unit>`\n- `getArticleDrafts()` -> `Result<List<ArticleDraft>>`\n\n### Step 4: String Resources\n**Modify**: `app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`\n- Strings for: new_article, compose_article, article_title_hint, article_content_hint, article_tags_hint, article_slug_hint, save_draft, publish, publishing, saving_draft, draft_saved, my_drafts, delete_draft, delete_draft_confirm, no_drafts, validation errors\n\n### Step 5: Article Editor ViewModel\n**Create**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleViewModel.kt`\n- State: title, content, tags, slug (auto from title), language, draftId, isSaving, isPublishing, isPublished, error\n- Methods: updateTitle (+auto-slug), updateContent, updateTags, updateSlug, saveDraft, publish, loadDraft(draftId), clearError\n\n### Step 6: Article Editor Screen\n**Create**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleScreen.kt`\n- Top bar: back + \"Write Article\"\n- Fields: title, tags, content (markdown), slug, language\n- Actions: Save Draft, Publish\n- Callbacks: `onPublishSuccess`, `onNavigateBack`\n\n### Step 7: Drafts List ViewModel\n**Create**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/drafts/DraftsViewModel.kt`\n- Load drafts list on init, delete draft, expose draft count\n\n### Step 8: Drafts List Screen\n**Create**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/drafts/DraftsScreen.kt`\n- List of draft cards: title, updated time, delete button\n- Delete confirmation dialog\n- Empty state\n- Tap draft -> navigate to article editor with draftId\n\n### Step 9: Navigation Routes\n**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/HackersPubApp.kt`\n- Add `DetailScreen.ComposeArticle(\"compose-article?draftId={draftId}\")`\n- Add `DetailScreen.Drafts(\"drafts\")`\n- Register composables, wire callbacks\n\n### Step 10: Timeline Top Bar — Article Icon with Badge\n**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/timeline/TimelineScreen.kt`\n- Add `onComposeArticleClick: () -> Unit` parameter\n- Add `draftCount: Int` parameter (from ViewModel or parent)\n- Add document icon in top bar with `BadgedBox` showing count when > 0\n- Same 28dp circle styling as existing icons\n\n### Step 11: Timeline ViewModel — Draft Count\n**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/timeline/TimelineViewModel.kt`\n- Fetch draft count on init (lightweight query or reuse drafts list)\n- Expose `draftCount` in UI state\n\n### Step 12: Settings — Drafts Entry\n**Modify**: Settings screen\n- Add \"My Drafts (N)\" row with count, navigates to Drafts screen\n\n## Files Summary\n\n**Create (4)**:\n- `app/.../ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleScreen.kt`\n- `app/.../ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleViewModel.kt`\n- `app/.../ui/screens/drafts/DraftsScreen.kt`\n- `app/.../ui/screens/drafts/DraftsViewModel.kt`\n\n**Modify (7)**:\n- `app/.../graphql/.../operations.graphql` — mutations + query\n- `app/.../domain/model/Models.kt` — data classes\n- `app/.../data/repository/HackersPubRepository.kt` — API methods\n- `app/.../res/values/strings.xml` — string resources\n- `app/.../ui/screens/timeline/TimelineScreen.kt` — top bar article icon + badge\n- `app/.../ui/screens/timeline/TimelineViewModel.kt` — draft count\n- `app/.../ui/HackersPubApp.kt` — routes + navigation wiring\n- Settings screen — drafts entry point\n\n## Verification\n\n1. Build after Step 1 for Apollo codegen\n2. Top bar shows article icon; badge appears when drafts exist\n3. Tap article icon -> blank article editor opens\n4. ## Approved Plan:
# Add "Create Article" Button to Home Screen

## Context

The home screen (TimelineScreen) has a single FAB for note creation. We need article creation (long-form with title, tags, markdown content) and draft management. The GraphQL schema already has `saveArticleDraft` and `deleteArticleDraft` mutations but no operations or UI exist yet. Publishing is out of scope — this is draft-save only.

## UX Decisions

- **FAB stays unchanged** — single tap goes to note compose (zero friction for common action)
- **Top bar icon** for articles — document icon in the top bar navigates directly to a blank article editor
- **Draft badge** on the article icon — shows draft count so user knows they have unfinished work
- **Drafts list** — standalone screen accessible from Settings ("My Drafts (N)") and by tapping the badge
- **No auto-save** — user explicitly taps "Save Draft"

## Screen Flows

### Timeline with Article Icon + Draft Badge

```
+----------------------------------+
| Timeline [doc(2)][👤+][⚙] avatar|
|                ^^^               |
|          badge = 2 drafts        |
|                                  |
|  +----------------------------+  |
|  | post...                    |  |
|  +----------------------------+  |
|                          [+ FAB] |
+----------------------------------+

[doc] tap (no drafts)  -> Article Editor (blank)
[doc] tap (has drafts) -> Article Editor (blank)
  * Badge is informational; tap always opens blank editor
  * User goes to Settings > My Drafts to resume drafts
[+ FAB] tap -> Compose Note (unchanged)
```

### Article Editor

```
+----------------------------------+
| <-  Write Article                |
|                                  |
| Title: [___________________]     |
| Tags:  [___________________]     |
|                                  |
| [                              ] |
| [   Markdown content area      ] |
| [                              ] |
|                                  |
|                    [Save Draft]  |
+----------------------------------+

"Save Draft" -> saveArticleDraft
             -> snackbar "Draft saved", stay on screen
```

### My Drafts Screen

```
+----------------------------------+
| <-  My Drafts                    |
|                                  |
| +------------------------------+ |
| | "My WIP article"             | |
| | Updated: 2 hours ago   [del] | |
| +------------------------------+ |
| | "Another idea"               | |
| | Updated: yesterday     [del] | |
| +------------------------------+ |
|                                  |
| (empty state: "No drafts yet")  |
+----------------------------------+

Tap draft -> Article Editor pre-filled with draft content
[del] -> confirm dialog -> deleteArticleDraft
```

### Settings Entry

```
Settings Screen:
+----------------------------------+
|  ...                             |
|  My Drafts (3)              >    |
|  ...                             |
+----------------------------------+

Tap -> navigate to My Drafts screen
```

## Implementation Steps

### Step 1: GraphQL Operations
**Modify**: `app/src/main/graphql/pub/hackers/android/operations.graphql`
- Add `SaveArticleDraft` mutation (title, content, tags, optional id)
- Add `DeleteArticleDraft` mutation (id)
- Add `ArticleDrafts` query (to list user's drafts)
- Follow union result type pattern from existing `CreateNote`

### Step 2: Domain Models
**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/domain/model/Models.kt`
- Add `ArticleDraft(id, title, content, tags, created, updated)`

### Step 3: Repository Methods
**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/data/repository/HackersPubRepository.kt`
- `saveArticleDraft(title, content, tags, id?)` -> `Result<ArticleDraft>`
- `deleteArticleDraft(id)` -> `Result<Unit>`
- `getArticleDrafts()` -> `Result<List<ArticleDraft>>`

### Step 4: String Resources
**Modify**: `app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`
- Strings for: new_article, compose_article, article_title_hint, article_content_hint, article_tags_hint, save_draft, saving_draft, draft_saved, my_drafts, delete_draft, delete_draft_confirm, no_drafts, validation errors

### Step 5: Article Editor ViewModel
**Create**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleViewModel.kt`
- State: title, content, tags, draftId, isSaving, error
- Methods: updateTitle, updateContent, updateTags, saveDraft, loadDraft(draftId), clearError

### Step 6: Article Editor Screen
**Create**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleScreen.kt`
- Top bar: back + "Write Article"
- Fields: title, tags, content (markdown)
- Action: Save Draft
- Callbacks: `onSaveSuccess`, `onNavigateBack`

### Step 7: Drafts List ViewModel
**Create**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/drafts/DraftsViewModel.kt`
- Load drafts list on init, delete draft, expose draft count

### Step 8: Drafts List Screen
**Create**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/drafts/DraftsScreen.kt`
- List of draft cards: title, updated time, delete button
- Delete confirmation dialog
- Empty state
- Tap draft -> navigate to article editor with draftId

### Step 9: Navigation Routes
**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/HackersPubApp.kt`
- Add `DetailScreen.ComposeArticle("compose-article?draftId={draftId}")`
- Add `DetailScreen.Drafts("drafts")`
- Register composables, wire `onSaveSuccess` and navigation callbacks

### Step 10: Timeline Top Bar — Article Icon with Badge
**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/timeline/TimelineScreen.kt`
- Add `onComposeArticleClick: () -> Unit` parameter
- Add `draftCount: Int` parameter (from ViewModel or parent)
- Add document icon in top bar with `BadgedBox` showing count when > 0
- Same 28dp circle styling as existing icons

### Step 11: Timeline ViewModel — Draft Count
**Modify**: `app/src/main/java/pub/hackers/android/ui/screens/timeline/TimelineViewModel.kt`
- Fetch draft count on init (lightweight query or reuse drafts list)
- Expose `draftCount` in UI state

### Step 12: Settings — Drafts Entry
**Modify**: Settings screen
- Add "My Drafts (N)" row with count, navigates to Drafts screen

## Files Summary

**Create (4)**:
- `app/.../ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleScreen.kt`
- `app/.../ui/screens/compose/ComposeArticleViewModel.kt`
- `app/.../ui/screens/drafts/DraftsScreen.kt`
- `app/.../ui/screens/drafts/DraftsViewModel.kt`

**Modify (7)**:
- `app/.../graphql/.../operations.graphql` — mutations + query
- `app/.../domain/model/Models.kt` — data classes
- `app/.../data/repository/HackersPubRepository.kt` — API methods
- `app/.../res/values/strings.xml` — string resources
- `app/.../ui/screens/timeline/TimelineScreen.kt` — top bar article icon + badge
- `app/.../ui/screens/timeline/TimelineViewModel.kt` — draft count
- `app/.../ui/HackersPubApp.kt` — routes + navigation wiring
- Settings screen — drafts entry point

## Commit Strategy (fine-grained)

1. `Add GraphQL operations for article draft management` — Steps 1
2. `Add ArticleDraft domain models` — Step 2
3. `Add article draft repository methods` — Step 3
4. `Add string resources for article creation and drafts` — Step 4
5. `Add ComposeArticleViewModel for article editor` — Step 5
6. `Add ComposeArticleScreen UI` — Step 6
7. `Add DraftsViewModel for drafts list` — Step 7
8. `Add DraftsScreen UI` — Step 8
9. `Add navigation routes for article editor and drafts` — Step 9
10. `Add article icon with draft badge to timeline top bar` — Steps 10-11
11. `Add My Drafts entry to Settings screen` — Step 12

## Verification

1. Build after Step 1 for Apollo codegen
2. Top bar shows article icon; badge appears when drafts exist
3. Tap article icon -> blank article editor opens
4. "Save Draft" -> snackbar "Draft saved", stays on editor, badge count increments
5. Settings > "My Drafts (N)" -> drafts list
6. Tap draft -> editor pre-filled with draft content
7. Delete draft -> confirm dialog -> removed from list, badge decrements
8. FAB still opens note compose (unchanged)

```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/kodingwarrior/fediverse/hackerspub-android-worktrees/feature-add-create-article/app/src/main/graphql/pub/hackers/android/operations.graphql",
  "old_string": "query RecommendedActors($limit: Int = 10) {\n    recommendedActors(limit: $limit) {\n        id\n        name\n        handle\n        avatarUrl\n        bio\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "query RecommendedActors($limit: Int = 10) {\n    recommendedActors(limit: $limit) {\n        id\n        name\n        handle\n        avatarUrl\n        bio\n    }\n}\n\nmutation SaveArticleDraft($title: String!, $content: Markdown!, $tags: [String!]!, $id: ID) {\n    saveArticleDraft(input: { title: $title, content: $content, tags: $tags, id: $id }) {\n        ... on SaveArticleDraftPayload {\n            draft {\n                id\n                title\n                content\n                tags\n                created\n                updated\n            }\n        }\n        ... on InvalidInputError {\n            inputPath\n        }\n        ... on NotAuthenticatedError {\n            notAuthenticated\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nmutation DeleteArticleDraft($id: ID!) {\n    deleteArticleDraft(input: { id: $id }) {\n        ... on DeleteArticleDraftPayload {\n            deletedDraftId\n        }\n        ... on InvalidInputError {\n            inputPath\n        }\n        ... on NotAuthenticatedError {\n            notAuthenticated\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nquery ArticleDrafts {\n    viewer {\n        id\n        articleDrafts(first: 50) {\n            edges {\n                node {\n                    id\n                    title\n                    content\n                    tags\n                    created\n                    updated\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nquery ArticleDraft($id: ID!) {\n    articleDraft(id: $id) {\n        id\n        title\n        content\n        tags\n        created\n        updated\n    }\n}"
}
```

674	    suspend fun sharePost(postId: String): Result<Unit> {
675	        return try {
676	            val response = apolloClient.mutation(SharePostMutation(postId)).execute()
677	
678	            if (response.hasErrors()) {
679	                Result.failure(Exception(response.errors?.firstOrNull()?.message ?: "Unknown error"))
680	            } else {
681	                val result = response.data?.sharePost
682	                when {
683	                    result?.onSharePostPayload != null -> Result.success(Unit)
684	                    result?.onInvalidInputError != null -> {

1148	    private fun NotificationsQuery.Node.toNotification(): Notification? {
1149	        val actors = actors.edges.map { it.node.actorFields.toActor() }
1150	        val created = Instant.parse(created.toString())
1151	
1152	        return when {
1153	            onFollowNotification != null -> Notification.Follow(
1154	                id = id,
1155	                uuid = uuid.toString(),
1156	                created = created,
1157	                actors = actors
1158	            )
1159	            onMentionNotification != null -> Notification.Mention(
1160	                id = id,
1161	                uuid = uuid.toString(),
1162	                created = created,
1163	                actors = actors,
1164	                post = onMentionNotification.post?.let { NotificationPost(id = it.id, content = it.content.toString()) }
1165	            )
1166	            onReplyNotification != null -> Notification.Reply(
1167	                id = id,
1168	                uuid = uuid.toString(),
1169	                created = created,
1170	                actors = actors,
1171	                post = onReplyNotification.post?.let { NotificationPost(id = it.id, content = it.content.toString()) }
1172	            )
1173	            onQuoteNotification != null -> Notification.Quote(
1174	                id = id,
1175	                uuid = uuid.toString(),
1176	                created = created,
1177	                actors = actors,
1178	                post = onQuoteNotification.post?.let { NotificationPost(id = it.id, content = it.content.toString()) }
1179	            )
1180	            onShareNotification != null -> Notification.Share(
1181	                id = id,
1182	                uuid = uuid.toString(),
1183	                created = created,
1184	                actors = actors,
1185	                post = onShareNotification.post?.let { NotificationPost(id = it.id, content = it.content.toString()) }
1186	            )
1187	            onReactNotification != null -> Notification.React(
1188	                id = id,
1189	                uuid = uuid.toString(),
1190	                created = created,
1191	                actors = actors,
1192	                emoji = onReactNotification.emoji,
1193	                customEmoji = onReactNotification.customEmoji?.let {
1194	                    CustomEmoji(
1195	                        id = it.id,
1196	                        name = it.name,
1197	                        imageUrl = it.imageUrl
1198	                    )
1199	                },
1200	                post = onReactNotification.post?.let { NotificationPost(id = it.id, content = it.content.toString()) }
1201	            )
1202	            else -> null
1203	        }
1204	    }

1206	    private fun GqlPostVisibility?.toPostVisibility(): PostVisibility {
1207	        return when (this) {
1208	            GqlPostVisibility.PUBLIC -> PostVisibility.PUBLIC
1209	            GqlPostVisibility.UNLISTED -> PostVisibility.UNLISTED
1210	            GqlPostVisibility.FOLLOWERS -> PostVisibility.FOLLOWERS
1211	            GqlPostVisibility.DIRECT -> PostVisibility.DIRECT
1212	            GqlPostVisibility.NONE -> PostVisibility.NONE
1213	            else -> PostVisibility.PUBLIC
1214	        }
1215	    }

1217	    private fun toJsonString(obj: Any?): String {
1218	        return when (obj) {
1219	            is Map<*, *> -> {
1220	                val jsonObj = org.json.JSONObject()
1221	                obj.forEach { (k, v) -> jsonObj.put(k.toString(), toJsonValue(v)) }
1222	                jsonObj.toString()
1223	            }
1224	            is List<*> -> {
1225	                val jsonArr = org.json.JSONArray()
1226	                obj.forEach { jsonArr.put(toJsonValue(it)) }
1227	                jsonArr.toString()
1228	            }
1229	            else -> obj.toString()
1230	        }
1231	    }
1232	
1233	    private fun toJsonValue(value: Any?): Any? {
1234	        return when (value) {
1235	            null -> org.json.JSONObject.NULL
1236	            is Map<*, *> -> {
1237	                val jsonObj = org.json.JSONObject()
1238	                value.forEach { (k, v) -> jsonObj.put(k.toString(), toJsonValue(v)) }
1239	                jsonObj
1240	            }
1241	            is List<*> -> {
1242	                val jsonArr = org.json.JSONArray()
1243	                value.forEach { jsonArr.put(toJsonValue(it)) }
1244	                jsonArr
1245	            }
1246	            else -> value
1247	        }
1248	    }

return try {\n            val response = apolloClient.mutation(\n                SaveArticleDraftMutation(\n                    title = title,\n                    content = content,\n                    tags = tags,\n                    id = Optional.presentIfNotNull(id)\n                )\n            ).execute()\n\n            if (response.hasErrors()) {\n                Result.failure(Exception(response.errors?.firstOrNull()?.message ?: \"Unknown error\"))\n            } else {\n                val result = response.data?.saveArticleDraft\n                when {\n                    result?.onSaveArticleDraftPayload != null -> {\n                        val draft = result.onSaveArticleDraftPayload.draft\n                        Result.success(\n                            ArticleDraft(\n                                id = draft.id,\n                                title = draft.title,\n                                content = draft.content.toString(),\n                                tags = draft.tags,\n                                created = Instant.parse(draft.created.toString()),\n                                updated = Instant.parse(draft.updated.toString())\n                            )\n                        )\n                    }\n                    result?.onInvalidInputError != null -> {\n                        Result.failure(Exception(\"Invalid input: ${result.onInvalidInputError.inputPath}\"))\n                    }\n                    result?.onNotAuthenticatedError != null -> {\n                        Result.failure(Exception(\"Not authenticated\"))\n                    }\n                    else -> Result.failure(Exception(\"Unknown error\"))\n                }\n            }\n        } catch (e: Exception) {\n            Result.failure(e)\n        }\n    }\n\n    suspend fun deleteArticleDraft(id: String): Result<Unit> {\n        return try {\n            val response = apolloClient.mutation(DeleteArticleDraftMutation(id)).execute()\n\n            if (response.hasErrors()) {\n                Result.failure(Exception(response.errors?.firstOrNull()?.message ?: \"Unknown error\"))\n            } else {\n                val result = response.data?.deleteArticleDraft\n                when {\n                    result?.onDeleteArticleDraftPayload != null -> Result.success(Unit)\n                    result?.onInvalidInputError != null ->\n                        Result.failure(Exception(\"Invalid input: ${result.onInvalidInputError.inputPath}\"))\n                    result?.onNotAuthenticatedError != null ->\n                        Result.failure(Exception(\"Not authenticated\"))\n                    else -> Result.failure(Exception(\"Unknown error\"))\n                }\n            }\n        } catch (e: Exception) {\n            Result.failure(e)\n        }\n    }\n\n    suspend fun getArticleDrafts(): Result<List<ArticleDraft>> {\n        return try {\n            val response = apolloClient.query(ArticleDraftsQuery())\n                .fetchPolicy(FetchPolicy.NetworkOnly)\n                .execute()\n\n            if (response.hasErrors()) {\n                Result.failure(Exception(response.errors?.firstOrNull()?.message ?: \"Unknown error\"))\n            } else {\n                val drafts = response.data?.viewer?.articleDrafts?.edges?.map { edge ->\n                    val node = edge.node\n                    ArticleDraft(\n                        id = node.id,\n                        title = node.title,\n                        content = node.content.toString(),\n                        tags = node.tags,\n                        created = Instant.parse(node.created.toString()),\n                        updated = Instant.parse(node.updated.toString())\n                    )\n                } ?: emptyList()\n                Result.success(drafts)\n            }\n        } catch (e: Exception) {\n            Result.failure(e)\n        }\n    }\n\n    suspend fun getArticleDraft(id: String): Result<ArticleDraft> {\n        return try {\n            val response = apolloClient.query(ArticleDraftQuery(id))\n                .fetchPolicy(FetchPolicy.NetworkOnly)\n                .execute()\n\n            if (response.hasErrors()) {\n                Result.failure(Exception(response.errors?.firstOrNull()?.message ?: \"Unknown error\"))\n            } else {\n                val draft = response.data?.articleDraft\n                    ?: return Result.failure(Exception(\"Draft not found\"))\n                Result.success(\n                    ArticleDraft(\n                        id = draft.id,\n                        title = draft.title,\n                        content = draft.content.toString(),\n                        tags = draft.tags,\n                        created = Instant.parse(draft.created.toString()),\n                        updated = Instant.parse(draft.updated.toString())\n                    )\n                )\n            }\n        } catch (e: Exception) {\n            Result.failure(e)\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Extension functions to convert GraphQL fragment types to domain models"
}

3	import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
4	import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
5	import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
6	import kotlinx.coroutines.FlowPreview
7	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableSharedFlow
8	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
9	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
10	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
11	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.collectLatest
12	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.debounce
13	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
14	import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
15	import android.icu.util.ULocale
16	import android.os.Build
17	import android.view.textclassifier.TextClassificationManager
18	import android.view.textclassifier.TextLanguage
19	import pub.hackers.android.data.repository.HackersPubRepository
20	import pub.hackers.android.domain.model.Actor
21	import pub.hackers.android.domain.model.Post
22	import pub.hackers.android.domain.model.PostVisibility
23	import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
24	import android.content.Context
25	import javax.inject.Inject
26	
27	data class ComposeUiState(
28	    val content: String = "",
29	    val cursorPosition: Int = 0,
30	    val language: String = java.util.Locale.getDefault().language,
31	    val visibility: PostVisibility = PostVisibility.PUBLIC,
32	    val replyToId: String? = null,
33	    val replyTargetPost: Post? = null,
34	    val isLoadingReplyTarget: Boolean = false,
35	    val quotedPostId: String? = null,
36	    val quotedPost: Post? = null,
37	    val isLoadingQuotedPost: Boolean = false,
38	    val quotedPostLoadFailed: Boolean = false,
39	    val isPosting: Boolean = false,
40	    val isPosted: Boolean = false,
41	    val error: String? = null,
42	    // Mention autocomplete state
43	    val mentionQuery: String? = null,
44	    val mentionStartIndex: Int = -1,
45	    val mentionSuggestions: List<Actor> = emptyList(),
46	    val isLoadingMentions: Boolean = false
47	)
48	
49	@OptIn(FlowPreview::class)
50	@HiltViewModel
51	class ComposeViewModel @Inject constructor(
52	    private val repository: HackersPubRepository,
53	    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context
54	) : ViewModel() {
55	
56	    private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow(ComposeUiState())
57	    val uiState: StateFlow<ComposeUiState> = _uiState.asStateFlow()
58	
59	    private var viewerHandle: String? = null
60	
61	    // Regex to detect @mention at cursor position
62	    // Matches: @handle or @user@domain patterns that are incomplete
63	    private val mentionRegex = Regex("""@([^\s@]+(?:@[^\s@]*)?)$""")
64	
65	    private val mentionQueryFlow = MutableSharedFlow<String>(replay = 0)
66	
67	    init {
68	        viewModelScope.launch {
69	            repository.getViewer().onSuccess { viewer ->
70	                viewerHandle = viewer?.handle
71	            }
72	        }
73	
74	        // Setup debounced mention search
75	        viewModelScope.launch {
76	            mentionQueryFlow
77	                .debounce(300L)
78	                .collectLatest { query ->
79	                    if (query.isNotEmpty()) {
80	                        searchMentions(query)
81	                    }
82	                }
83	        }
84	    }
85	
86	    private suspend fun searchMentions(query: String) {
87	        _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoadingMentions = true) }
88	
89	        repository.searchActorsByHandle(query, limit = 10)
90	            .onSuccess { actors ->
91	                _uiState.update {
92	                    it.copy(
93	                        mentionSuggestions = actors,
94	                        isLoadingMentions = false
95	                    )
96	                }
97	            }
98	            .onFailure {
99	                _uiState.update {
100	                    it.copy(
101	                        mentionSuggestions = emptyList(),
102	                        isLoadingMentions = false
103	                    )
104	                }
105	            }
106	    }
107	
108	    private fun detectMentionTrigger(content: String, cursorPosition: Int) {
109	        // Get text before cursor
110	        val textBeforeCursor = content.take(cursorPosition)
111	
112	        // Find mention pattern at cursor position
113	        val match = mentionRegex.find(textBeforeCursor)
114	
115	        if (match != null) {
116	            val mentionQuery = match.groupValues[1]
117	            val mentionStartIndex = match.range.first
118	
119	            _uiState.update {
120	                it.copy(
121	                    mentionQuery = mentionQuery,
122	                    mentionStartIndex = mentionStartIndex
123	                )
124	            }
125	
126	            // Trigger debounced search
127	            viewModelScope.launch {
128	                mentionQueryFlow.emit(mentionQuery)
129	            }
130	        } else {
131	            // Clear mention state
132	            clearMentionState()
133	        }
134	    }
135	
136	    private fun clearMentionState() {
137	        _uiState.update {
138	            it.copy(
139	                mentionQuery = null,
140	                mentionStartIndex = -1,
141	                mentionSuggestions = emptyList(),
142	                isLoadingMentions = false
143	            )
144	        }
145	    }
146	
147	    fun selectMention(actor: Actor): Pair<String, Int> {
148	        val state = _uiState.value
149	        val content = state.content
150	        val startIndex = state.mentionStartIndex
151	
152	        if (startIndex >= 0) {
153	            // Replace @partial with @full_handle
154	            val beforeMention = content.take(startIndex)
155	            val textAfterMentionStart = content.drop(startIndex)
156	
157	            // Find where the partial mention ends (next space, newline, or end of string)
158	            val mentionEndOffset = textAfterMentionStart.indexOfFirst { it == ' ' || it == '\n' }
159	            val afterMention = if (mentionEndOffset >= 0) {
160	                textAfterMentionStart.drop(mentionEndOffset)
161	            } else {
162	                " " // Add space after mention if at end
163	            }
164	
165	            val normalizedHandle = actor.handle.removePrefix("@")
166	            val insertedHandle = "@$normalizedHandle "
167	            val newContent = "$beforeMention$insertedHandle${afterMention.trimStart()}"
168	            val newCursorPosition = beforeMention.length + insertedHandle.length
169	
170	            _uiState.update {
171	                it.copy(
172	                    content = newContent,
173	                    cursorPosition = newCursorPosition,
174	                    mentionQuery = null,
175	                    mentionStartIndex = -1,
176	                    mentionSuggestions = emptyList(),
177	                    isLoadingMentions = false
178	                )
179	            }
180	
181	            return Pair(newContent, newCursorPosition)
182	        }
183	
184	        return Pair(content, state.cursorPosition)
185	    }
186	
187	    fun dismissMentionSuggestions() {
188	        clearMentionState()
189	    }
190	
191	    fun setQuotedPost(postId: String) {
192	        _uiState.update { it.copy(quotedPostId = postId, isLoadingQuotedPost = true, quotedPostLoadFailed = false) }
193	        viewModelScope.launch {
194	            repository.getPostDetail(postId)
195	                .onSuccess { result ->
196	                    _uiState.update {
197	                        it.copy(
198	                            quotedPost = result.post,
199	                            isLoadingQuotedPost = false
200	                        )
201	                    }
202	                }
203	                .onFailure {
204	                    _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoadingQuotedPost = false, quotedPostLoadFailed = true) }
205	                }
206	        }
207	    }
208	
209	    fun setReplyTarget(postId: String) {
210	        _uiState.update { it.copy(replyToId = postId, isLoadingReplyTarget = true) }
211	        viewModelScope.launch {
212	            repository.getPostDetail(postId)
213	                .onSuccess { result ->
214	                    val post = result.post
215	                    val mentionPrefix = buildMentionPrefix(post)
216	                    _uiState.update {
217	                        it.copy(
218	                            replyTargetPost = post,
219	                            isLoadingReplyTarget = false,
220	                            content = mentionPrefix,
221	                            cursorPosition = mentionPrefix.length
222	                        )
223	                    }
224	                }
225	                .onFailure {
226	                    _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoadingReplyTarget = false) }
227	                }
228	        }
229	    }
230	
231	    private fun buildMentionPrefix(post: Post): String {
232	        val mentions = mutableSetOf<String>()
233	
234	        // Add the post author (normalize by removing leading @)
235	        mentions.add(post.actor.handle.removePrefix("@"))
236	
237	        // Add existing mentions from the post (normalize)
238	        mentions.addAll(post.mentions.map { it.removePrefix("@") })
239	
240	        // Remove viewer's own handle if present (normalize for comparison)
241	        viewerHandle?.let { mentions.remove(it.removePrefix("@")) }
242	
243	        return if (mentions.isNotEmpty()) {
244	            mentions.joinToString(" ") { "@${it.removePrefix("@")}" } + " "
245	        } else {
246	            ""
247	        }
248	    }
249	
250	    fun updateContent(content: String, cursorPosition: Int = content.length) {
251	        _uiState.update { it.copy(content = content, cursorPosition = cursorPosition) }
252	
253	        // Check for mention trigger
254	        detectMentionTrigger(content, cursorPosition)
255	
256	        // Detect language
257	        detectLanguage(content)
258	    }
259	
260	    private fun detectLanguage(text: String) {
261	        if (text.isBlank() || text.length < 20) return
262	
263	        try {
264	            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
265	                val tcm = context.getSystemService(Context.TEXT_CLASSIFICATION_SERVICE) as? TextClassificationManager
266	                val classifier = tcm?.textClassifier ?: return
267	                val request = TextLanguage.Request.Builder(text).build()
268	                val result = classifier.detectLanguage(request)
269	                if (result.localeHypothesisCount > 0) {
270	                    val topLocale = result.getLocale(0)
271	                    val lang = topLocale.language
272	                    if (lang.isNotEmpty()) {
273	                        _uiState.update { it.copy(language = lang) }
274	                    }
275	                }
276	            }
277	        } catch (_: Exception) {
278	            // Fallback: keep current language
279	        }
280	    }
281	
282	    fun updateVisibility(visibility: PostVisibility) {
283	        _uiState.update { it.copy(visibility = visibility) }
284	    }
285	
286	    fun post() {
287	        val state = _uiState.value
288	        if (state.content.isBlank() || state.isPosting) return
289	
290	        viewModelScope.launch {
291	            _uiState.update { it.copy(isPosting = true, error = null) }
292	
293	            repository.createNote(
294	                content = state.content,
295	                language = state.language,
296	                visibility = state.visibility,
297	                replyTargetId = state.replyToId,
298	                quotedPostId = state.quotedPostId
299	            )
300	                .onSuccess {
301	                    _uiState.update { it.copy(isPosting = false, isPosted = true) }
302	                }
303	                .onFailure { error ->
304	                    _uiState.update {
305	                        it.copy(
306	                            error = error.message,
307	                            isPosting = false
308	                        )
309	                    }
310	                }
311	        }
312	    }
313	
314	    fun clearError() {
315	        _uiState.update { it.copy(error = null) }
316	    }
317	}
318	
```

87	@Composable
88	fun ComposeScreen(
89	    replyToId: String?,
90	    quotedPostId: String? = null,
91	    onPostSuccess: () -> Unit,
92	    onNavigateBack: () -> Unit,
93	    viewModel: ComposeViewModel = hiltViewModel()
94	) {
95	    val uiState by viewModel.uiState.collectAsState()
96	    val snackbarHostState = remember { SnackbarHostState() }
97	    var showVisibilityMenu by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
98	
99	    val colors = LocalAppColors.current
100	    val typography = LocalAppTypography.current
101	
102	    // Track TextFieldValue for cursor position
103	    var textFieldValue by remember {
104	        mutableStateOf(TextFieldValue(text = uiState.content))
105	    }
106	
107	    // Track cursor and text field positions for popup placement
108	    var cursorRect by remember { mutableStateOf(Rect.Zero) }
109	    var textFieldBounds by remember { mutableStateOf(Rect.Zero) }
110	    var textLayoutResult by remember { mutableStateOf<TextLayoutResult?>(null) }
111	    val scrollState = rememberScrollState()
112	    val focusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
113	    val keyboardController = LocalSoftwareKeyboardController.current
114	    val density = LocalDensity.current
115	    val popupHeight = with(density) { 200.dp.toPx() } // Estimated popup height
116	    val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
117	
118	    // Auto-scroll to keep cursor visible when text overflows
119	    LaunchedEffect(cursorRect) {
120	        if (cursorRect != Rect.Zero) {
121	            val cursorBottom = cursorRect.bottom.toInt()
122	            val cursorTop = cursorRect.top.toInt()
123	            val viewportTop = scrollState.value
124	            val viewportBottom = viewportTop + scrollState.viewportSize
125	
126	            if (cursorBottom > viewportBottom) {
127	                scrollState.animateScrollTo(cursorBottom - scrollState.viewportSize)
128	            } else if (cursorTop < viewportTop) {
129	                scrollState.animateScrollTo(cursorTop)
130	            }
131	        }
132	    }
133	
134	    // Sync TextFieldValue with ViewModel state changes (e.g., when mention is selected)
135	    LaunchedEffect(uiState.content, uiState.cursorPosition) {
136	        if (textFieldValue.text != uiState.content) {
137	            textFieldValue = TextFieldValue(
138	                text = uiState.content,
139	                selection = TextRange(uiState.cursorPosition)
140	            )
141	        }
142	    }
143	
144	    LaunchedEffect(replyToId) {
145	        replyToId?.let { viewModel.setReplyTarget(it) }
146	    }
147	
148	    LaunchedEffect(quotedPostId) {
149	        quotedPostId?.let { viewModel.setQuotedPost(it) }
150	    }
151	
152	    LaunchedEffect(uiState.isPosted) {
153	        if (uiState.isPosted) {
154	            onPostSuccess()
155	        }
156	    }
157	
158	    LaunchedEffect(uiState.error) {
159	        uiState.error?.let {
160	            snackbarHostState.showSnackbar(it)
161	            viewModel.clearError()
162	        }
163	    }
164	
165	    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
166	        focusRequester.requestFocus()
167	    }
168

203	                .fillMaxSize()

219	                    // Custom text field with cursor position tracking

224	                                textFieldBounds = coordinates.boundsInWindow()
225	                            },

232	                                .fillMaxSize()
233	                                .clickable(

238	                                    keyboardController?.show()
239	                                }
240	                                .padding(16.dp)
241	                                .verticalScroll(scrollState)
242	                        ) {
243	                            BasicTextField(
244	                                value = textFieldValue,
245	                                onValueChange = { newValue: TextFieldValue ->
246	                                    textFieldValue = newValue
247	                                    viewModel.updateContent(
248	                                        content = newValue.text,
249	                                        cursorPosition = newValue.selection.start
250	                                    )
251	                                },
252	                                onTextLayout = { result: TextLayoutResult ->
253	                                    textLayoutResult = result
254	                                    // Update cursor position
255	                                    val cursorPos = textFieldValue.selection.start
256	                                        .coerceIn(0, textFieldValue.text.length)
257	                                    cursorRect =
258	                                        if (textFieldValue.text.isNotEmpty() || cursorPos == 0) {
259	                                            result.getCursorRect(cursorPos)
260	                                        } else {
261	                                            Rect.Zero
262	                                        }
263	                                },
264	                                modifier = Modifier
265	                                    .fillMaxWidth()
266	                                    .focusRequester(focusRequester),
267	                                enabled = !uiState.isPosting,

272	                                decorationBox = { innerTextField ->

275	                                            Text(

282	                                    }
283	                                }
284	                            )
285	                        }
286	                    }

288	                    // Mention autocomplete popup

290	                        // Calculate cursor position accounting for padding and scroll

292	                        val cursorYInBox = cursorRect.bottom - scrollState.value + paddingPx
293	                        val cursorXInBox = cursorRect.left + paddingPx
294	
295	                        // Clamp to visible area
296	                        val visibleCursorY =
297	                            cursorYInBox.coerceIn(paddingPx, textFieldBounds.height - paddingPx)
298	
299	                        Popup(

305	                                        .coerceAtLeast(0)
306	                                ),
307	                                y = (visibleCursorY + with(density) { 20.dp.toPx() }).roundToInt()
308	                            ),

322	                            )

324	                    }

334	
335	            // Visibility toolbar pinned above keyboard

337	            Row(
338	                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
339	                modifier = Modifier
340	                    .fillMaxWidth()
341	                    .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
342	            ) {
343	                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
344	
345	                TextButton(
346	                    onClick = { showVisibilityMenu = true },
347	                    contentPadding = androidx.compose.foundation.layout.PaddingValues(

362	                            PostVisibility.FOLLOWERS -> stringResource(R.string.visibility_followers)
363	                            else -> stringResource(R.string.visibility_public)
364	                        },
365	                        tint = colors.textSecondary
366	                    )
367	                    Icon(

369	                        contentDescription = null,
370	                        tint = colors.textSecondary,
371	                        modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
372	                    )
373	
374	                    DropdownMenu(

461	private fun ReplyTargetPreview(

  "content": "package pub.hackers.android.ui.screens.drafts\n\nimport androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel\nimport androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope\nimport dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.launch\nimport pub.hackers.android.data.repository.HackersPubRepository\nimport pub.hackers.android.domain.model.ArticleDraft\nimport javax.inject.Inject\n\ndata class DraftsUiState(\n    val drafts: List<ArticleDraft> = emptyList(),\n    val isLoading: Boolean = false,\n    val error: String? = null,\n    val deletingDraftId: String? = null\n)\n\n@HiltViewModel\nclass DraftsViewModel @Inject constructor(\n    private val repository: HackersPubRepository\n) : ViewModel() {\n\n    private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow(DraftsUiState())\n    val uiState: StateFlow<DraftsUiState> = _uiState.asStateFlow()\n\n    init {\n        loadDrafts()\n    }\n\n    fun loadDrafts() {\n        viewModelScope.launch {\n            _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoading = true, error = null) }\n\n            repository.getArticleDrafts()\n                .onSuccess { drafts ->\n                    _uiState.update {\n                        it.copy(\n                            drafts = drafts,\n                            isLoading = false\n                        )\n                    }\n                }\n                .onFailure { error ->\n                    _uiState.update {\n                        it.copy(\n                            error = error.message,\n                            isLoading = false\n                        )\n                    }\n                }\n        }\n    }\n\n    fun deleteDraft(draftId: String) {\n        viewModelScope.launch {\n            _uiState.update { it.copy(deletingDraftId = draftId) }\n\n            repository.deleteArticleDraft(draftId)\n                .onSuccess {\n                    _uiState.update { state ->\n                        state.copy(\n                            drafts = state.drafts.filter { it.id != draftId },\n                            deletingDraftId = null\n                        )\n                    }\n                }\n                .onFailure { error ->\n                    _uiState.update {\n                        it.copy(\n                            error = error.message,\n                            deletingDraftId = null\n                        )\n                    }\n                }\n        }\n    }\n\n    fun clearError() {\n        _uiState.update { it.copy(error = null) }\n    }\n}\n"
}

     }\n        )\n    }\n\n    Scaffold(\n        contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0),\n        topBar = {\n            LargeTitleHeader(\n                title = stringResource(R.string.my_drafts),\n                leadingContent = {\n                    IconButton(onClick = onNavigateBack) {\n                        Icon(\n              )\n                    }\n                }\n            )\n        },\n        snackbarHost = { SnackbarHost(snackbarHostState) }\n    ) { paddingValues ->\n        Box(\n uiState.error ?: stringResource(R.string.error_generic),\n                        onRetry = { viewModel.loadDrafts() }\n                    )\n                }\n LazyColumn(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {\n                        items(uiState.drafts, key = { it.id }) { draft ->\n                            DraftItem(\n  onDeleteClick = { draftToDelete = draft }\n                            )\n                            HorizontalDivider(color = colors.divider)\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n@Composable\nprivate fun DraftItem(\n    draft: ArticleDraft,\n    isDeleting: Boolean,\n    onClick: () -> Unit,\n    onDeleteClick: () -> Unit\n) FormatStyle.SHORT)\n            .withZone(ZoneId.systemDefault())\n    }\n\n    Row(\n        modifier = Modifier\n            .fillMaxWidth()\n            .clickable(enabled = !isDeleting, onClick = onClick)\n            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),\n        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically\n    ) {\n        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {\n            Text(\n                text = draft.title.ifBlank { \"(Untitled)\" },\n                style = typography.titleMedium,\n                color = colors.textPrimary,\n                maxLines = 1,\n                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis\n            )\n            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))\n            Text(\n                text = dateFormatter.format(draft.updated),\n                style = typography.bodySmall,\n                color = colors.textSecondary\n            )\n            if (draft.tags.isNotEmpty()) {\n                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))\n                Text(\n                    text = draft.tags.joinToString(\", \"),\n                    style = typography.bodySmall,\n                    color = colors.textSecondary,\n                    maxLines = 1,\n                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis\n                )\n            }\n        }\n\n        IconButton(\n            onClick = onDeleteClick,\n            enabled = !isDeleting,\n            modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp)\n        ) {\n            Icon(\n                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Delete,\n                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.delete_draft),\n                tint = if (isDeleting) colors.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.3f) else colors.textSecondary\n            )\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

102	sealed class DetailScreen(val route: String) {
103	    data object SignIn : DetailScreen("signin?token={token}&code={code}") {
104	        fun createRoute(token: String? = null, code: String? = null): String {
105	            val params = mutableListOf<String>()
106	            if (token != null) params.add("token=$token")
107	            if (code != null) params.add("code=$code")
108	            return if (params.isEmpty()) "signin" else "signin?${params.joinToString("&")}"
109	        }
110	    }
111	    data object Compose : DetailScreen("compose?replyTo={replyTo}&quoteOf={quoteOf}") {
112	        fun createRoute(replyTo: String? = null, quoteOf: String? = null): String {
113	            val params = mutableListOf<String>()
114	            if (replyTo != null) params.add("replyTo=$replyTo")
115	            if (quoteOf != null) params.add("quoteOf=$quoteOf")
116	            return if (params.isEmpty()) "compose" else "compose?${params.joinToString("&")}"
117	        }
118	    }

120	        fun createRoute(postId: String) = "post/$postId"
121	    }

123	        fun createRoute(handle: String) = "profile/$handle"
124	    }

1	package pub.hackers.android.ui.screens.timeline

3	import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
4	import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
5	import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
