7	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
8	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
9	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
10	import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
11	import pub.hackers.android.data.local.PreferencesManager
12	import pub.hackers.android.data.repository.HackersPubRepository
13	import pub.hackers.android.domain.model.Post
14	import pub.hackers.android.domain.model.ReactionGroup
15	import java.time.Instant
16	import javax.inject.Inject
17	
18	data class TimelineUiState(
19	    val posts: List<Post> = emptyList(),
20	    val isLoading: Boolean = false,
21	    val isRefreshing: Boolean = false,
22	    val isLoadingMore: Boolean = false,
23	    val hasNextPage: Boolean = false,
24	    val endCursor: String? = null,
25	    val error: String? = null,
26	    val reactionPickerPostId: String? = null
27	)
28	
29	@HiltViewModel
30	class TimelineViewModel @Inject constructor(
31	    private val repository: HackersPubRepository,
32	    val preferencesManager: PreferencesManager
33	) : ViewModel() {
34	
35	    private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow(TimelineUiState())
36	    val uiState: StateFlow<TimelineUiState> = _uiState.asStateFlow()
37	
38	    private var lastLoadTime: Instant? = null
39	    private val staleThresholdSeconds = 60L
40	
41	    init {
42	        loadTimeline()
43	    }
44	
45	    fun refreshIfStale() {
46	        val last = lastLoadTime ?: return
47	        if (Instant.now().epochSecond - last.epochSecond > staleThresholdSeconds) {
48	            refresh()
49	        }
50	    }
51	
52	    private fun loadTimeline() {
53	        viewModelScope.launch {
54	            _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoading = true, error = null) }
55	
56	            repository.getPersonalTimeline(refresh = true)
57	                .onSuccess { result ->
58	                    lastLoadTime = Instant.now()
59	                    _uiState.update {
60	                        it.copy(
61	                            posts = result.posts,
62	                            hasNextPage = result.hasNextPage,
63	                            endCursor = result.endCursor,
64	                            isLoading = false
65	                        )
66	                    }
67	                }
68	                .onFailure { error ->
69	                    _uiState.update {
70	                        it.copy(
71	                            error = error.message,
72	                            isLoading = false
73	                        )
74	                    }
75	                }
76	        }
77	    }
78	
79	    fun refresh() {
80	        viewModelScope.launch {
81	            _uiState.update { it.copy(isRefreshing = true, error = null) }
82	
83	            repository.getPersonalTimeline(refresh = true)
84	                .onSuccess { result ->
85	                    lastLoadTime = Instant.now()
86	                    _uiState.update {
87	                        it.copy(
88	                            posts = result.posts,
89	                            hasNextPage = result.hasNextPage,
90	                            endCursor = result.endCursor,
91	                            isRefreshing = false
92	                        )
93	                    }
94	                }
95	                .onFailure { error ->
96	                    _uiState.update {
97	                        it.copy(
98	                            error = error.message,
99	                            isRefreshing = false
100	                        )
101	                    }
102	                }
103	        }
104	    }
105	
106	    fun loadMore() {
107	        val currentState = _uiState.value
108	        if (!currentState.hasNextPage || currentState.isLoadingMore) return
109	
110	        viewModelScope.launch {
111	            _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoadingMore = true) }
112	
113	            repository.getPersonalTimeline(after = currentState.endCursor)
114	                .onSuccess { result ->
115	                    _uiState.update {
116	                        it.copy(
117	                            posts = (it.posts + result.posts).distinctBy { p -> p.id },
118	                            hasNextPage = result.hasNextPage,
119	                            endCursor = result.endCursor,
120	                            isLoadingMore = false
121	                        )
122	                    }
123	                }
124	                .onFailure {
125	                    _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoadingMore = false) }
126	                }
127	        }
128	    }
129	
130	    fun sharePost(postId: String) {
131	        // Optimistic update
132	        _uiState.update { state ->
133	            state.copy(
134	                posts = state.posts.map { post ->
135	                    if (post.id == postId) {
136	                        post.copy(
137	                            viewerHasShared = true,
138	                            engagementStats = post.engagementStats.copy(
139	                                shares = post.engagementStats.shares + 1
140	                            )
141	                        )
142	                    } else post
143	                }
144	            )
145	        }
146	        viewModelScope.launch {
147	            repository.sharePost(postId)
148	                .onFailure {
149	                    // Revert on failure
150	                    _uiState.update { state ->
151	                        state.copy(
152	                            posts = state.posts.map { post ->
153	                                if (post.id == postId) {
154	                                    post.copy(
155	                                        viewerHasShared = false,
156	                                        engagementStats = post.engagementStats.copy(
157	                                            shares = maxOf(0, post.engagementStats.shares - 1)
158	                                        )
159	                                    )
160	                                } else post
161	                            }
162	                        )
163	                    }
164	                }
165	        }
166	    }
167	
168	    fun unsharePost(postId: String) {
169	        // Optimistic update
170	        _uiState.update { state ->
171	            state.copy(
172	                posts = state.posts.map { post ->
173	                    if (post.id == postId) {
174	                        post.copy(
175	                            viewerHasShared = false,
176	                            engagementStats = post.engagementStats.copy(
177	                                shares = maxOf(0, post.engagementStats.shares - 1)
178	                            )
179	                        )
180	                    } else post
181	                }
182	            )
183	        }
184	        viewModelScope.launch {
185	            repository.unsharePost(postId)
186	                .onFailure {
187	                    // Revert on failure
188	                    _uiState.update { state ->
189	                        state.copy(
190	                            posts = state.posts.map { post ->
191	                                if (post.id == postId) {
192	                                    post.copy(
193	                                        viewerHasShared = true,
194	                                        engagementStats = post.engagementStats.copy(
195	                                            shares = post.engagementStats.shares + 1
196	                                        )
197	                                    )
198	                                } else post
199	                            }
200	                        )
201	                    }
202	                }
203	        }
204	    }
205	
206	    fun toggleFavourite(postId: String) {
207	        toggleReaction(postId, "❤️")
208	    }
209	
210	    fun toggleReaction(postId: String, emoji: String) {
211	        val post = _uiState.value.posts.find {
212	            it.id == postId || it.sharedPost?.id == postId
213	        } ?: return
214	        val targetPost = post.sharedPost ?: post
215	        val existingGroup = targetPost.reactionGroups.find { it.emoji == emoji }
216	        val viewerHasReacted = existingGroup?.viewerHasReacted == true
217	
218	        // Optimistic update
219	        _uiState.update { state ->
220	            state.copy(
221	                posts = state.posts.map { p ->
222	                    val target = p.sharedPost ?: p
223	                    if (target.id == targetPost.id) {
224	                        val updatedGroups = updateReactionGroups(
225	                            target.reactionGroups, emoji, viewerHasReacted
226	                        )
227	                        val totalReactions = updatedGroups.sumOf { it.count }
228	                        val updatedTarget = target.copy(
229	                            reactionGroups = updatedGroups,
230	                            engagementStats = target.engagementStats.copy(
231	                                reactions = totalReactions
232	                            )
233	                        )
234	                        if (p.sharedPost != null) p.copy(sharedPost = updatedTarget)
235	                        else updatedTarget
236	                    } else p
237	                },
238	                reactionPickerPostId = null
239	            )
240	        }
241	
242	        viewModelScope.launch {
243	            val result = if (viewerHasReacted) {
244	                repository.removeReactionFromPost(targetPost.id, emoji)
245	            } else {
246	                repository.addReactionToPost(targetPost.id, emoji)
247	            }
248	
249	            result.onFailure {
250	                // Revert on failure
251	                _uiState.update { state ->
252	                    state.copy(
253	                        posts = state.posts.map { p ->
254	                            val target = p.sharedPost ?: p
255	                            if (target.id == targetPost.id) {
256	                                val revertedGroups = updateReactionGroups(
257	                                    target.reactionGroups, emoji, !viewerHasReacted
258	                                )
259	                                val totalReactions = revertedGroups.sumOf { it.count }
260	                                val revertedTarget = target.copy(
261	                                    reactionGroups = revertedGroups,
262	                                    engagementStats = target.engagementStats.copy(
263	                                        reactions = totalReactions
264	                                    )
265	                                )
266	                                if (p.sharedPost != null) p.copy(sharedPost = revertedTarget)
267	                                else revertedTarget
268	                            } else p
269	                        }
270	                    )
271	                }
272	            }
273	        }
274	    }
275	
276	    fun showReactionPicker(postId: String) {
277	        _uiState.update { it.copy(reactionPickerPostId = postId) }
278	    }
279	
280	    fun hideReactionPicker() {
281	        _uiState.update { it.copy(reactionPickerPostId = null) }
282	    }
283	
284	    private fun updateReactionGroups(
285	        groups: List<ReactionGroup>,
286	        emoji: String,
287	        wasReacted: Boolean
288	    ): List<ReactionGroup> {
289	        return if (wasReacted) {
290	            groups.map { group ->
291	                if (group.emoji == emoji) {
292	                    group.copy(count = maxOf(0, group.count - 1), viewerHasReacted = false)
293	                } else group
294	            }.filter { it.count > 0 || it.viewerHasReacted }
295	        } else {
296	            val existing = groups.find { it.emoji == emoji }
297	            if (existing != null) {
298	                groups.map { group ->
299	                    if (group.emoji == emoji) {
300	                        group.copy(count = group.count + 1, viewerHasReacted = true)
301	                    } else group
302	                }
303	            } else {
304	                groups + ReactionGroup(
305	                    emoji = emoji, customEmoji = null,
306	                    count = 1, reactors = emptyList(), viewerHasReacted = true
307	                )
308	            }
309	        }
310	    }
311	}
312

181	                        color = colors.textPrimary
182	                    )
183	                }
184	
185	                if (passkeyEnabled) {
186	                    HorizontalDivider(color = colors.divider, thickness = 1.dp)
187	
188	                    // Passkeys section
189	                    Row(
190	                        modifier = Modifier
191	                            .fillMaxWidth()
192	                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
193	                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
194	                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
195	                    ) {
196	                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
197	                            Icon(
198	                                imageVector = Icons.Default.Fingerprint,
199	                                contentDescription = null,
200	                                tint = colors.textSecondary
201	                            )
202	                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
203	                            Text(
204	                                text = stringResource(R.string.passkeys),
205	                                style = typography.bodyLarge,
206	                                color = colors.textPrimary
207	                            )
208	                        }
209	                        IconButton(onClick = { showAddPasskeyDialog = true }) {
210	                            Icon(
211	                                imageVector = Icons.Default.Add,
212	                                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.add_passkey),
213	                                tint = colors.accent
214	                            )
215	                        }
216	                    }
217	
218	                    if (uiState.isLoadingPasskeys) {
219	                        Box(
220	                            modifier = Modifier
221	                                .fillMaxWidth()
222	                                .padding(16.dp),
223	                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
224	                        ) {
225	                            CircularProgressIndicator(modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp))
226	                        }
227	                    } else if (uiState.passkeys.isEmpty()) {
228	                        Text(
229	                            text = stringResource(R.string.no_passkeys),
230	                            style = typography.bodyMedium,
231	                            color = colors.textSecondary,
232	                            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 56.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
233	                        )
234	                    } else {
235	                        uiState.passkeys.forEach { passkey ->
236	                            Row(
237	                                modifier = Modifier
238	                                    .fillMaxWidth()
239	                                    .padding(start = 56.dp, end = 8.dp, top = 4.dp, bottom = 4.dp),
240	                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
241	                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
242	                            ) {
243	                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
244	                                    Text(
245	                                        text = passkey.name,
246	                                        style = typography.bodyMedium,
247	                                        color = colors.textPrimary
248	                                    )
249	                                    Text(
250	                                        text = passkey.created,
251	                                        style = typography.labelSmall,
252	                                        color = colors.textSecondary
253	                                    )
254	                                }
255	                                IconButton(onClick = { showRevokePasskeyId = passkey.id }) {
256	                                    Icon(
257	                                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Delete,
258	                                        contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.remove),
259	                                        tint = colors.reaction
260	                                    )
261	                                }
262	                            }
263	                        }
264	                    }
265	                } // passkeyEnabled
266	            } else {
267	                Row(
268	                    modifier = Modifier
269	                        .fillMaxWidth()
270	                        .clickable(onClick = onSignInClick)
271	                        .padding(16.dp),
272	                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
273	                ) {
274	                    Icon(
275	                        imageVector = Icons.Filled.Login,
276	                        contentDescription = null,
277	                        tint = colors.textSecondary
278	                    )
279	                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
280	                    Text(
281	                        text = stringResource(R.string.sign_in),
282	                        style = typography.bodyLarge,
283	                        color = colors.textPrimary
284	                    )
285	                }
286	            }
287	
288	            HorizontalDivider(color = colors.divider, thickness = 1.dp)
289	
290	            Row(
291	                modifier = Modifier
292	                    .fillMaxWidth()
293	                    .clickable { viewModel.clearCache() }
294	                    .padding(16.dp),
295	                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
296	            ) {
297	                Icon(
298	                    imageVector = Icons.Filled.Delete,
299	                    contentDescription = null,
300	                    tint = colors.textSecondary
301	                )
302	                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
303	                Text(
304	                    text = stringResource(R.string.clear_cache),
305	                    style = typography.bodyLarge,
306	                    color = colors.textPrimary
307	                )
308	            }
309	
310	            HorizontalDivider(color = colors.divider, thickness = 1.dp)
311	
312	            Row(
313	                modifier = Modifier
314	                    .fillMaxWidth()
315	                    .padding(16.dp),
316	                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
317	            ) {
318	                Icon(
319	                    imageVector = Icons.Filled.Info,
320	                    contentDescription = null,
321	                    tint = colors.textSecondary
322	                )
323	                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
324	                Column {
325	                    Text(
326	                        text = stringResource(R.string.about),
327	                        style = typography.bodyLarge,
328	                        color = colors.textPrimary
329	                    )
330	                    Text(
331	                        text = "${stringResource(R.string.version)} ${uiState.appVersion}",
332	                        style = typography.bodyMedium,
333	                        color = colors.textSecondary
334	                    )
335	                }
336	            }
337	        }
338	    }
339	
340	    // Add passkey dialog
341	    if (passkeyEnabled && showAddPasskeyDialog) {
342	        var passkeyName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
343	        val activity = LocalContext.current as Activity
344	
345	        AlertDialog(
346	            onDismissRequest = { showAddPasskeyDialog = false },
347	            title = { Text(stringResource(R.string.add_passkey)) },
348	            text = {
349	                OutlinedTextField(
350	                    value = passkeyName,
351	                    onValueChange = { passkeyName = it },
352	                    placeholder = { Text(stringResource(R.string.passkey_name_hint)) },
353	                    singleLine = true,
354	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
355	                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
356	                        focusedBorderColor = colors.accent,
357	                        cursorColor = colors.accent
358	                    )
359	                )
360	            },
361	            confirmButton = {
362	                TextButton(
363	                    onClick = {
364	                        showAddPasskeyDialog = false
365	                        viewModel.registerPasskey(passkeyName.ifBlank { "Android" }, activity)
366	                    },
367	                    enabled = !uiState.isRegisteringPasskey
368	                ) {
369	                    Text(stringResource(R.string.add))
370	                }
371	            },
372	            dismissButton = {
373	                TextButton(onClick = { showAddPasskeyDialog = false }) {
374	                    Text(stringResource(R.string.cancel))
375	                }
376	            }
377	        )
378	    }
379	
380	    // Revoke passkey confirmation dialog
381	    val passkeyIdToRevoke = showRevokePasskeyId
382	    if (passkeyEnabled && passkeyIdToRevoke != null) {
383	        RevokePasskeyDialog(
384	            passkeyId = passkeyIdToRevoke,
385	            onConfirm = { id ->
386	                viewModel.revokePasskey(id)
387	                showRevokePasskeyId = null
388	            },
389	            onDismiss = { showRevokePasskeyId = null }
390	        )
391	    }
392	}
393	
394	@Composable
395	@androidx.annotation.VisibleForTesting
396	internal fun RevokePasskeyDialog(
397	    passkeyId: String,
398	    onConfirm: (String) -> Unit,
399	    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
400	) {
401	    val colors = LocalAppColors.current
402	    AlertDialog(
403	        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
404	        title = { Text(stringResource(R.string.remove_passkey)) },
405	        text = { Text(stringResource(R.string.remove_passkey_confirm)) },
406	        confirmButton = {
407	            TextButton(onClick = { onConfirm(passkeyId) }) {
408	                Text(stringResource(R.string.remove), color = colors.reaction)
409	            }
410	        },
411	        dismissButton = {
412	            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
413	                Text(stringResource(R.string.cancel))
414	            }
415	        }
416	    )
417	}
418

> AGENT

All 11 commits are in place. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

**Feature: Create Article + Draft Management**

| Commit | What |
|--------|------|
| `eabd6f2` | GraphQL operations: `SaveArticleDraft`, `DeleteArticleDraft`, `ArticleDrafts`, `ArticleDraft` |
| `6972b19` | `ArticleDraft` domain model |
| `9f9b709` | Repository methods: save, delete, list, get drafts |
| `3ac8814` | String resources for article creation and drafts |
| `1aeaf19` | `ComposeArticleViewModel` — title/content/tags state, save draft logic |
| `3e17da8` | `ComposeArticleScreen` — full editor UI with Save Draft button |
| `c5241ec` | `DraftsViewModel` — load/delete drafts list |
| `f704c76` | `DraftsScreen` — drafts list with delete confirmation |
| `9deadb5` | Navigation routes wired in `HackersPubApp.kt` |
| `fa0bd32` | Article icon + draft badge in timeline top bar |
| `fc49225` | "My Drafts" entry in Settings screen |

**UX flow:**
- **FAB** still opens note compose (unchanged)
- **Article icon** (with draft count badge) in the top bar opens a blank article editor
- **Article editor** has title, tags, and markdown content fields with a "Save Draft" button
- **Settings > My Drafts** shows a list of saved drafts — tap to edit, delete with confirmation
- Draft count refreshes when returning to the timeline
